- **agent-team-os**: Hire workers, not tools. /ledger measures your week in your own words. /match derives the team those numbers justify and lists what nothing here does. /onboard builds the repo end to end, from a blank GitHub account to a dashboard on your phone. /routines snapshots what is really scheduled on your account and /arm settles every job into armed or off-with-a-reason, so nothing is left pretending to run. /new-agent adds a specialist, /new-workflow turns a conversation into a named scheduled job, /new-skill adds one named capability without scheduling anything, /add-pack installs a ready-made one… ([AutomatedMarketer/agent-team-os](https://github.com/AutomatedMarketer/agent-team-os/tree/HEAD/agent-team-os) · 12 skills)
